            total_amount = amounts[0]\n        \n        # Pattern 2: Amount due\n        elif re.search(r'^\\s*Amount\\s+due\\s*:?\\s*\\$', line, re.IGNORECASE):\n            amounts = re.findall(r'\\$\\s*([0-9,.\\s]+)', line)\n            if amounts:\n                total_amount = amounts[0]\n        \n        # Pattern 3: \"Gross worth\" keyword, amount might be on same line with $ or next line\n        elif re.search(r'Gross\\s+worth', line, re.IGNORECASE):\n            # Look for $ amount on this line\n            amounts = re.findall(r'\\$\\s*([0-9,.\\s]+)', line)\n            if amounts:\n                total_amount = amounts[0]\n            # Or look on the next 2 lines\n            elif not total_amount:\n                for j in range(i+1, min(i+3, len(lines))):\n                    if '$' in lines[j]:\n                        amounts = re.findall(r'\\$\\s*([0-9,.\\s]+)', lines[j])\n                        if amounts:\n                            total_amount = amounts[0]\n                            break\n                    elif re.search(r'^\\s*[0-9,.\\s]+\\s*$', lines[j]):\n                        total_amount = lines[j].strip()\n                        break\n        \n        # Pattern 4: VAT line\n        elif re.search(r'^\\s*VAT\\s*$', line, re.IGNORECASE):\n            if '[%]' not in line:\n                if i + 1 < len(lines) and '$' in lines[i+1]:\n                    amounts = re.findall(r'\\$\\s*([0-9,.\\s]+)', lines[i+1])\n                    if amounts:\n                        vat_amount = amounts[0]\n                elif '$' total_amount = normalize_amount(total_amount) if total_amount else None\n    vat_amount = normalize_amount(vat_amount) if vat_amount else None\n    \n    return total_amount, vat_amount or \"\"\n\n#
